### List words (#339)

A token that is a one-word term of the Greek lists, as written or lowercased, keys that term, itself when it is a headword, and of its lemmas only the verbs (αυτό keys αυτό, not αυτός; πάω keys πάω and πηγαίνω). Other tokens key their lemmas and themselves as headwords, as before. A term's spellings are the term and its cleaned spellings (στη(ν): στη, στην). A list word that is no verb shows its own forms first; a verb keeps one sentence per form, and its own form leads when that pass leaves it out.

- One-word terms of `ky-ell`: 4,525; with an example: 2,820; with 3 or more: 1,947; every example in the term's own form: 1,463
- List words whose examples differ from the plain per-form choice: 903; non-verbs, own forms first: 852; verbs whose dropped own form leads: έρχομαι, ακολουθώ, αλλάζω, αφήνω, βάζω, βγάζω, βγαίνω, βοηθάω, βρίσκω, δίνω, δείχνω, επισκέπτομαι, επιστρέφω, ζητάω, ζω, θυμώνω, καλώ, καπνίζω, κλαίω, κρατάω, κόβω, λέω, λείπω, μιλάω, μπαίνω, νοιάζομαι, ξεκινάω, οδηγώ, πέφτω, παρακολουθώ, περνάω, περπατάω, πηγαίνω, πλένω, πονάω, ρίχνω, σηκώνω, σκοτώνω, στέλνω, σταματάω, συμφωνώ, συνηθίζω, τελειώνω, τραγουδάω, υπάρχω, υποστηρίζω, φέρνω, φεύγω, φοράω, φτάνω, φτιάχνω

### Morphy ambiguity (CodeRabbit, #177)

Morphy's suffix rules over-generate (0136; evening → even, singing → singe). A token keys no inferred lemma when two of its lemmas are list words (lives: live, life), nor a lemma a suffix rule guessed when the token is itself a list word (evening, building); WordNet's exception lists stay trusted (was → be, saw → see). The rules compared on the same candidates:

| Rule | List words with an example | With 3 or more | Tokens whose inferred keys are skipped | List words whose examples differ from no rule |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| no inferred key when two are list words, nor a suffix guess for a list word (built) | 7,496 | 6,662 | 306 | 278 |
| no inferred key when two or more are list words | 7,498 | 6,677 | 11 | 19 |
| an inferred key only as the token's one list-word key, the token included | 7,496 | 6,661 | 380 | 332 |
| no rule | 7,499 | 6,679 | 0 | 0 |

The 60 most frequent of the 306 tokens whose inferred keys are skipped, with their morphy lemmas and occurrences in the candidates: does (do, doe; 5,697), better (good, well; 3,677), doing (do; 3,565), used (use; 2,618), best (good, well; 1,918), being (be; 1,787), tired (tire; 1,629), lives (live, life; 1,593), working (work; 1,430), trying (try; 1,413), times (time; 1,242), living (live; 1,193), reading (read; 1,116), meeting (meet; 1,076), later (late; 988), surprised (surprise; 939), interesting (interest; 897), scared (scare; 742), learning (learn; 735), thanks (thank; 727), interested (interest; 716), closed (close; 705), smoking (smoke; 679), evening (even; 616), feeling (feel; 607), building (build; 603), glasses (glass; 600), writing (write; 593), means (mean; 509), expected (expect; 484), walking (walk; 444), leaves (leave, leaf; 405), united (unite; 400), fishing (fish; 394), singing (sing, singe; 393), convinced (convince; 373), bored (bore; 331), cooking (cook; 330), missing (miss; 323), dressed (dress; 322), standing (stand; 319), injured (injure; 297), disappointed (disappoint; 296), boring (bore; 291), moving (move; 286), painting (paint; 281), prepared (prepare; 280), dating (date; 266), sports (sport; 257), abandoned (abandon; 250), pleased (please; 243), fixed (fix; 225), teaching (teach; 222), following (follow; 214), meaning (mean; 209), dancing (dance; 207), hearing (hear; 201), complicated (complicate; 197), divorced (divorce; 196), fighting (fight; 194).

## Drill cells (#364, #365)

The cells: every cell the conjugation drill can ask (`drill_cells.py`, a port of 0132's rules: the three Greek lists' verbs, every tense up to B2, both voices). A cell's sentence holds the cell's whole form by 0134's match rule and is keyed `<word>|<facet>|<person>` in `example_word`. Every cell of the coverage gate's scope (Verbs up to B1, tenses up to B1 (2.1)) has one sentence or is skipped; the gate does not cover the cells outside it, such as the B2 tenses', which can have neither a sentence nor a skip entry. Sources, in this order: Tatoeba (#364), Wiktionary usage examples (#365), sentences from openly licensed corpora (#385) and generated sentences (#365), the last three from `Scripts/data/drill-examples/`. "Skipped": cells with no natural sentence, listed in `ell-skipped.tsv`, which the coverage gate counts as done. "Waiting for a reading verdict": open cells whose found sentences hold them only through a spelling of another reading (match rule 5), which takes a sentence only with an `ok` verdict of the second pass.

- The pool: 595 verbs: A1 196, A2 66, B1 333; 102 with passive forms only, 4 impersonal
- Cells: 56,728; with a sentence: 27,417 (48.3%); sentences: 27,172, of them already word examples: 1,801, new: 25,371
- Homographs: aspect-only cells (one sentence serves both) 345; cells with a spelling of another reading 4,285, of them with a sentence through that spelling (an `ok` verdict, or a data row written for the cell) 2,164, through another of their forms 296, waiting for a reading verdict 239; reading verdicts in `ell-readings.tsv`: ok 3,729, no 1,348
- Spellings in the cells of two verbs (43): έχει φερθεί, έχεις φερθεί, έχετε φερθεί, έχουμε φερθεί, έχουν φερθεί, έχω φερθεί, είχα φερθεί, είχαμε φερθεί, είχαν φερθεί, είχατε φερθεί, είχε φερθεί, είχες φερθεί, θα έχει φερθεί, θα έχεις φερθεί, θα έχετε φερθεί, θα έχουμε φερθεί, θα έχουν φερθεί, θα έχω φερθεί, θα φερθεί, θα φερθείς, θα φερθείτε, θα φερθούμε, θα φερθούν, θα φερθούνε, θα φερθώ, να φερθεί, να φερθείς, να φερθείτε, να φερθούμε, να φερθούν, να φερθούνε, να φερθώ, πέστε, πιέστε, φέρθηκα, φέρθηκαν, φέρθηκε, φέρθηκες, φερθήκαμε, φερθήκαν, φερθήκανε, φερθήκατε, φερθείτε.

### Skipped cells (4,869)

The cells with no natural sentence (kyukhin 2026-11-22), with the second pass's reason, those in the coverage gate's scope (Verbs up to B1, tenses up to B1 (2.1)) first. A cell whose own corpus row ships says so; its line is the fallback should the row drop (#385).

| Cell | In scope | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| έρχομαι\|imperativeContinuous.passive\|2pl | yes | rejected by the check: Pattern Z: an open invitation is Να έρχεστε όποτε θέλετε or Ελάτε; a bare Έρχεστε reads as the present "you come whenever you like", so no natural sentence shows this cell. |
| αγαπάω\|futureContinuous.passive\|1sg | yes | passive εγώ "I will be loved": the personal passive of αγαπάω lives in set να phrases (να αγαπώ και να αγαπιέμαι); for the future Greek says «θα με αγαπάνε» (active 3pl), and the reciprocal needs a plural |
